**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/SearchParameter/location-address
**Summary**: Server SHALL support search parameter 'address' on Location resource
**Requirement**: "Select Locations with the specified address"
**Context**: The Plan-Net server must support searching for Location resources using the 'address' search parameter of type string. This parameter searches on Location.address and supports multipleAnd (parameter may repeat to specify multiple values that must all be true) and multipleOr (parameter may have multiple values separated by comma where at least one must be true). Allowed modifiers are 'exact' and 'contains'.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: False
**Conditionality Details**: NA
**Verifiable**: Yes
**Verifiability Details**: Automation
**Section**: 11.20.1 SearchParameter: Plannet_sp_location_address
**Grouping**: Location

This section describes the structure and definition of the "Via Intermediary" extension, including its data type constraints, cardinality, and context of use. However, it does not contain specific testable requirements with conformance verbs (SHALL, SHOULD, MAY, etc.) that would be suitable for extraction as INCOSE-style requirements.

The text is primarily descriptive and structural in nature, documenting:
- The extension's URL and version
- Its purpose and description
- Where it may be used (ContactPoint element)
- The allowed reference types (PractitionerRole, Organization, OrganizationAffiliation, Location)
- Cardinality constraints (0..*, 1..1)

None of these constitute behavioral requirements that can be tested against an actor. They are structural definitions that would be validated through conformance to the FHIR structure definition itself, rather than through integration testing of system behavior.## REQ-001

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/STU1.2/implementation.html
**Summary**: Plan-Net service SHALL NOT require consumer identifying information
**Requirement**: "A conformant Plan-Net service SHALL NOT require a directory mobile application to send consumer identifying information in order to query content."
**Context**: Privacy considerations for Plan-Net services require that access to the directory should not require authentication and should not associate consumers with their queries. This requirement ensures that the server does not demand consumer-identifiable information from applications querying the directory.
**Conformance**: SHALL NOT

**Section**: 7.1.2 Privacy Considerations
**Grouping**: Privacy

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition-contactpoint-availabletime.html
**Summary**: Contactpoint Availabletime Extension daysOfWeek value binding requirement
**Requirement**: "To be conformant, the concept in this element SHALL be from the specified value set [DaysOfWeek (http://hl7.org/fhir/ValueSet/days-of-week)]"
**Context**: The contactpoint-availabletime extension includes a daysOfWeek sub-extension with a code datatype value. This requirement constrains the allowed code values to those defined in the FHIR standard DaysOfWeek value set, ensuring interoperability and consistency in representing days of the week when specifying contact point availability.

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition/newpatients
**Summary**: New Patients Extension SHALL include acceptingPatients element with required binding
**Requirement**: "Extension.extension:acceptingPatients.value[x] SHALL be from the specified value set AcceptingPatientsVS (required conformance)"
**Context**: The New Patients extension, which may be used on HealthcareService, Location, and PractitionerRole resources, includes a mandatory acceptingPatients element that indicates whether new patients are being accepted. This element has a required binding to the AcceptingPatientsVS value set, meaning conformant implementations must use codes from this specific value set.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: False
**Conditionality Details**: NA
**Verifiable**: Yes
**Verifiability Details**: Automation
**Section**: 11.73.1 Extension: New Patients - Terminology Bindings
**Grouping**: General/Cross-Resource

## REQ-002

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition/newpatients
**Summary**: New Patients Extension constraint - no characteristics allowed when not accepting patients
**Requirement**: "If no new patients are accepted, no characteristics are allowed: extension.where(url='acceptingPatients').value.ofType(CodeableConcept).coding.where(code = 'no') implies extension.where(url='characteristics').empty()"
**Context**: The New Patients extension includes a constraint (new-patients-characteristics) that enforces business logic: when the acceptingPatients element has a code value of 'no', the characteristics extension element must be empty. This prevents contradictory data where a provider indicates they are not accepting new patients but also specifies characteristics of patients they would accept.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: True
**Conditionality Details**: This constraint only applies when extension.where(url='acceptingPatients').value.ofType(CodeableConcept).coding.where(code = 'no') evaluates to true
**Verifiable**: Yes
**Verifiability Details**: Automation
**Section**: 11.73.1 Extension: New Patients - Constraints
**Grouping**: General/Cross-ResourceI have carefully analyzed the provided FHIR Implementation Guide text. This appears to be the home/introduction page of the implementation guide, which contains background information, organizational details, dependencies, and intellectual property considerations.

After thorough review, I found that this section does not contain specific, testable requirements with conformance verbs (SHALL, SHOULD, MAY, etc.) that describe behavior or capabilities that can be objectively verified through software testing or attestation.

The content includes:
- Introduction and overview information
- Organizational structure of the IG
- Conceptual relationships and diagrams
- Dependency lists
- Intellectual property and licensing statements
- Background and context

None of these constitute testable requirements according to INCOSE standards for this Healthcare Integration Test Engineering context.No testable requirements with explicit conformance language (SHALL, SHOULD, MAY, etc.) were found in the provided text. The content describes a ValueSet definition for HealthcareService Types, which is a terminology artifact that lists permissible codes but does not contain behavioral or capability requirements that can be tested.I have carefully analyzed the provided FHIR Implementation Guide text. This section is an "Artifacts Summary" page that provides an inventory and catalog of the various FHIR artifacts (capability statements, search parameters, profiles, extensions, value sets, code systems, and examples) defined in the implementation guide.

This page is primarily informational and descriptive in nature. It lists and describes the artifacts but does not contain specific, testable requirements with conformance verbs (SHALL, SHOULD, MAY) that constrain system behavior. The text provides summaries and descriptions of what each artifact represents, but these are definitional statements about the artifacts themselves rather than requirements for system implementation or behavior.

The only statement that approaches a requirement is: "Systems conforming to this implementation guide are expected to declare conformance to one or more of the following capability statements." However, this is a general expectation statement that lacks the specificity and testable nature needed for extraction as a standalone requirement according to INCOSE standards.

All other content consists of artifact listings, descriptions, and navigation elements that do not constitute testable requirements for system behavior.## REQ-001

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition-qualification.html
**Summary**: Qualification Extension - Code Element Cardinality
**Requirement**: "[The Qualification extension] extension:code [has cardinality] 1.1"
**Context**: The Qualification extension is used to add qualifications for an organization (e.g., accreditation) or practitionerRole (e.g., registered to prescribe controlled substances). The extension can be applied to Organization, OrganizationAffiliation, and PractitionerRole resources. The code element within this extension is mandatory and must appear exactly once, representing the type of qualification.

## REQ-004

**URL**: 
**Summary**: Practitioner NPI must be 10 digits
**Requirement**: "NPI must be 10 digits (value.matches('^[0-9]{10}$'))"
**Context**: When a Practitioner identifier is sliced as an NPI (National Provider Identifier), the value must match the pattern of exactly 10 numeric digits. This is enforced through the invariant us-core-16.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: True
**Conditionality Details**: Only applies when the identifier is an NPI (identifier:NPI slice)

**Grouping**: Practitioner

## REQ-005

**URL**: 
**Summary**: Practitioner NPI check digit must be valid
**Requirement**: "NPI check digit must be valid (Luhn algorithm check) ((((select(value.substring(0,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(1,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(2,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(3,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(4,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(5,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(6,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(7,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(8,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(9,1).toInteger()) + 24)mod 10=0))"
**Context**: When a Practitioner identifier is sliced as an NPI, the check digit must pass the Luhn algorithm validation. This is enforced through the invariant us-core-17 to ensure the NPI is mathematically valid.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: True
**Conditionality Details**: Only applies when the identifier is an NPI (identifier:NPI slice)
**Verifiable**: Yes
**Verifiability Details**: Automation
**Section**: 11.64.1 Resource Profile: PlannetPractitioner - Detailed Descriptions
**Grouping**: Practitioner

**Requirement**: "If the resource is contained in another resource, it SHALL be referred to from elsewhere in the resource or SHALL refer to the containing resource (contained.where((('#'+id in (%resource.descendants().reference   %resource.descendants().as(canonical)   %resource.descendants().as(uri)   %resource.descendants().as(url))) or descendants().where(reference = '#').exists() or descendants().where(as(canonical) = '#').exists() or descendants().where(as(canonical) = '#').exists()).not()).trace('unmatched', id).empty())"

**Requirement**: "Applications updating a name SHALL ensure that when both text and parts are present, no content is included in the text that isn't found in a part."

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition/plannet-Network
**Summary**: Plan-Net Network Organization active element must be true
**Requirement**: "[The Plan-Net Network Organization] active [element has a] Fixed Value: true"
**Context**: The Plan-Net Network profile constrains the Organization resource to represent healthcare provider insurance networks. The active element indicates whether the organization's record is still in active use. This requirement mandates that all Plan-Net Network Organization instances must have the active flag set to true.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: False
**Conditionality Details**: NA
**Verifiable**: Yes
**Verifiability Details**: Automation
**Section**: 11.61.1 Resource Profile: Plan-Net Network
**Grouping**: Organization

## REQ-002

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition/plannet-Network
**Summary**: Plan-Net Network Organization type must be from NetworkTypeVS value set
**Requirement**: "[The Plan-Net Network Organization] type [element has cardinality] 1.1 [and] Binding: Network Type VS ([required])"
**Context**: The type element identifies the kind of organization. For Plan-Net Network Organizations, this must be a value from the NetworkTypeVS value set to properly classify the type of healthcare provider insurance network. The required binding strength means implementations must use a code from this value set.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: False
**Conditionality Details**: NA
**Verifiable**: Yes
**Verifiability Details**: Automation
**Section**: 11.61.1 Resource Profile: Plan-Net Network
**Grouping**: Organization

## REQ-003

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition/plannet-Network
**Summary**: Plan-Net Network Organization must have a partOf reference to Plan-Net Organization
**Requirement**: "[The Plan-Net Network Organization] partOf [element has cardinality] 1.1 [and type] Reference(Plan-Net Organization) [and description] The organization that manages this network"
**Context**: The partOf element establishes the relationship between the network and the managing organization (typically a payer). This is a mandatory element that must reference a Plan-Net Organization resource, establishing the ownership/management relationship of the network.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: False
**Conditionality Details**: NA
**Verifiable**: Yes
**Verifiability Details**: Automation
**Section**: 11.61.1 Resource Profile: Plan-Net Network
**Grouping**: Organization

## REQ-004

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition/plannet-Network
**Summary**: Plan-Net Network NPI identifier must be 10 digits
**Requirement**: "NPI must be 10 digits: value.matches('^[0-9]{10}$')"
**Context**: When a Plan-Net Network Organization includes an NPI (National Provider Identifier) in the identifier element, the value must conform to the standard 10-digit format. This constraint (us-core-16) validates the format of the NPI identifier value.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: True
**Conditionality Details**: This requirement applies when the Organization includes an NPI identifier (identifier:NPI slice).
**Verifiable**: Yes
**Verifiability Details**: Automation
**Section**: 11.61.1.1.7 Constraints
**Grouping**: Organization

## REQ-005

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition/plannet-Network
**Summary**: Plan-Net Network NPI identifier check digit must be valid per Luhn algorithm
**Requirement**: "NPI check digit must be valid (Luhn algorithm check): (((select(value.substring(0,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(1,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(2,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(3,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(4,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(5,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(6,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(7,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(8,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(9,1).toInteger()) + 24)mod 10=0)"
**Context**: When a Plan-Net Network Organization includes an NPI identifier, the check digit (last digit) must be mathematically valid according to the Luhn algorithm. This constraint (us-core-17) ensures the integrity of the NPI value.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: True
**Conditionality Details**: This requirement applies when the Organization includes an NPI identifier (identifier:NPI slice).
**Verifiable**: Yes
**Verifiability Details**: Automation
**Section**: 11.61.1.1.7 Constraints
**Grouping**: Organization

## REQ-006

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition/plannet-Network
**Summary**: Plan-Net Network CLIA identifier must be 10 digits with letter D in third position
**Requirement**: "CLIA number must be 10 digits with a letter 'D' in third position: value.matches('^[0-9]{2}D[0-9]{7}$')"
**Context**: When a Plan-Net Network Organization includes a CLIA (Clinical Laboratory Improvement Amendments) identifier, the value must conform to the standard format of 10 characters with a letter 'D' in the third position. This constraint (us-core-18) validates the format of CLIA identifiers.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: True
**Conditionality Details**: This requirement applies when the Organization includes a CLIA identifier (identifier:CLIA slice).
**Verifiable**: Yes
**Verifiability Details**: Automation
**Section**: 11.61.1.1.7 Constraints
**Grouping**: Organization

**Context**: This is a validation constraint (us-core-16) that requires NPI values to be exactly 10 numeric digits. This ensures compliance with the NPI format standards.
**Conformance**: SHALL

## REQ-PRAC-006

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition/plannet-Practitioner
**Summary**: NPI check digit must be valid (Luhn algorithm)
**Requirement**: "NPI check digit must be valid (Luhn algorithm check): (((select(value.substring(0,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(1,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(2,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(3,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(4,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(5,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(6,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(7,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(8,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(9,1).toInteger()) + 24)mod 10=0)"
**Context**: This is a validation constraint (us-core-17) that requires NPI identifiers to have a valid check digit according to the Luhn algorithm, which is the standard validation algorithm for NPIs.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: True
**Conditionality Details**: This requirement applies when an NPI identifier is provided for a Practitioner.
**Verifiable**: Yes
**Verifiability Details**: Automation
**Section**: 11.64.1 Resource Profile: Plan-Net Practitioner - Constraints
**Grouping**: Practitioner

## REQ-ORG-006

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition/plannet-Organization
**Summary**: NPI check digit must be valid using Luhn algorithm
**Requirement**: "NPI check digit must be valid (Luhn algorithm check): (((select(value.substring(0,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(1,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(2,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(3,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(4,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(5,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(6,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(7,1).toInteger())+(select(value.substring(8,1).toInteger()).select(iif($this<5, $this*2, (($this*2)-9))))+(value.substring(9,1).toInteger()) + 24)mod 10=0)"
**Context**: For Organization resources with an NPI identifier slice, the NPI value must pass Luhn algorithm validation to ensure the check digit is correct.
**Conformance**: SHALL
**Actor**: Health Plan API Actor
**Sub-Requirement(s)**: NA
**Conditionality**: True
**Conditionality Details**: This requirement applies only if an NPI identifier is provided
**Verifiable**: Yes
**Verifiability Details**: Automation
**Section**: 11.62.1.1.10 Constraints
**Grouping**: Organization

**Grouping**: Organization

## REQ-002

**URL**: http://hl7.org/fhir/us/davinci-pdex-plan-net/StructureDefinition/plannet-PractitionerRole
**Summary**: PractitionerRole must have at least one of practitioner, organization, healthcareService, or location
**Requirement**: "If PlannetPractitionerRole.practitioner is absent (PlannetPractitionerRole.organization, PlannetPractitionerRole.healthcareservice, PlannetPractitionerRole.location) must be present: practitioner.exists() or (organization.exists() or healthcareService.exists() or location.exists())"

This constraint ensures that the role has sufficient context for understanding who or what entity is performing the role.
